# PORT_MAP — C# → Rust correspondence

The single deliberate gap in the graphs: graphify indexes the C# source and the Rust
target **separately** and cannot link them across languages. This file *is* that link.
Keep it current — a wrong or missing row is the fastest way for an agent to
re-implement something that already exists, or to port the wrong file.

- **Source of truth (C#):** `decompiled/` (Terraria 1.4.5.8) and `decompiled-libs/ReLogic`
  (the game's own library — `.xnb`/LZX/DXT content, IO, OS, utilities).
- **Target (Rust):** `crates/`.
- **Graphs:** Terraria, ReLogic and the Rust target are each indexed, plus a merged graph
  of all three; see [`AGENTS.md`](../AGENTS.md) → "Knowledge graph (graphify)". In the
  merged graph ids are tagged `decompiled::`, `decompiled-libs::` and `repo::`.

**State legend:** `done` = ported and tested · `partial` = core present, gaps remain ·
`wip` = actively being ported · `todo` = not started · `n/a` = deliberately not ported.

> **Known player-facing defects:** the 2026-11-10 audit (20 reported bugs, each with the
> exact diverging C# and Rust sites) lives in [`BUG_REPORT.md`](BUG_REPORT.md); the
> claimable fix rows are in [`ROADMAP.md`](ROADMAP.md) → "Known bugs — player audit".

## Not ported (by design)

| C# source | Why |
| --- | --- |
| `Social/*` (Steam, Discord, etc.) | platform integrations; not needed for the core port. **S1-I-6:** the transport is TCP-only by design (`net.rs:3021-3022`/`:3330-3331`, `lobby_id = 0`) while the wire is the vanilla's; the full divergence (unchanged protocol vs the lost lobby/NAT/invite/vanilla-interop) is recorded in `docs/ANALYSIS.md` "The Steam transport divergence" |
| `BCrypt*` | third-party password hashing |
| `Achievements/`, `Initializers/` platform hooks | platform-specific |
| `Achievements/Achievement.cs` + `AchievementManager.cs` + `GameContent/Achievements/*Condition.cs` + `Initializers/AchievementInitializer.cs` + `GameContent/UI/States/UIAchievementsMenu.cs` + `GameContent/UI/Elements/UIAchievementListItem.cs` | `crates/tr-app/src/achievements.rs` (runtime, save path/completion hook, notify wrappers, the per-row `completed_events` queue) + `achievements_data.rs` (137 rows / 180 conditions, `CreateMany` expanded) + `ui/achievements.rs` (menu/list rows) + notify sites in `app/saveglue.rs`, `world_sim/{clock,editing,npcs,weather,player,wiring}.rs`, `chest.rs`, `shimmer.rs` | partial (M6-ACH-2 menu/registry; S1-E-18 save + expanded conditions + selected progression/custom/int/float sites; remaining rows blocked on missing systems / S1-F-15) |
| `UI/InGameNotificationsTracker.cs` + `UI/InGamePopups.cs::AchievementUnlockedPopup` + `Initializers/AchievementInitializer.cs:728-735` (the completion subscriber) + `UI/IngameFancyUI.cs::OpenAchievementsAndGoto` + `UIList.Goto` | `crates/tr-app/src/ui/notifications.rs` (the tracker + popup: timer/curves/draw/click), `app/mod.rs::drain_achievement_completions` (the event seam + chat/sound subscriber), `app/clicks.rs::open_achievements_and_goto`, the update tick in `app/frame.rs` (`Main.cs:17045`), the draw in `app/frame/draw.rs` (`DrawInterface_13`), `saveglue.rs::save_and_quit`'s `Clear` | done (S1-E-26 wave A; the join-request popup + notifications-area draw unported — ANALYSIS "The achievement unlock popup") |

## How to extend this file

When you start or finish a system:

1. Find the C# entry point in the matching graph:
   - Terraria: `cd decompiled && graphify explain "Terraria/<File>.cs::<Symbol>"`.
   - ReLogic: `cd decompiled-libs && graphify explain "<File>.cs::<Symbol>"` (paths are
     relative to `decompiled-libs/`, e.g. `ReLogic/ReLogic.Content/ContentManager.cs`).
2. Confirm the Rust counterpart exists once (not twice):
   `graphify query "<symbol or concept>" --graph graphify-out/merged-graph.json`.
3. Add a row here mapping **C# file(s) → Rust file(s)** and set the state.

Prefer one row per coherent system over one row per class — this file is a map, not a
line-by-line index (the graphs already give you file + line).
| `WorldBuilding/WorldGenerationOptions.cs` + the nine `WorldSeedOption_*.cs` (the classic secret-seed registry), `IO/WorldFileData.cs`'s `TranslateSeed`/`Crc32`/`GetSerializedSeedsSum`/`GetFullSeedText`, `WorldGen.Reset`'s flag set (`:11109-11144`), the pass-level seed arms (TileRunner `:76772-77091`, evil `:13938-14304`, spider/moss/shroom `:17378`/`:17503`/`:9802`/`:79866`, dungeon/trees/ocean-caves `:14804`/`:15504`/`:15178`, Wavy Caves + `LavaLayerCaverer` `:12271`/`:14867`, remix spawn `:19574`) and the `Finish*` tails (`FinishDrunkGen` `:26226`, `FinishGetGoodWorld` `:25799`, `FinishTenthAnniversaryWorld` `:24237`) | `crates/tr-core/src/seeds.rs` (the `OPTION_TABLE`, `SeedFlags`, the codec, the serialiser, the config line) + `crates/tr-core/src/world.rs`/`worldgen/mod.rs` (`World.seeds`, `GenOptions.flags`) + `worldgen/pipeline.rs` (`GenVars.crimson_left`/`flip_infections`, the `wavy caves`/`constant lava layer`/`seed finish` steps) + `worldgen/tile_runner.rs`/`caves.rs`/`evil.rs`/`spider_gem_moss.rs`/`biomes.rs`/`dungeon.rs`/`living_trees.rs`/`ocean.rs`/`wavy.rs`/`finish_seeds.rs` (the arms) + `save.rs` (TRWLD v6) + `wld.rs` (`load_header_flags`) + `tr-app/src/ui/world_creation.rs` (`seed_flags`) + `tr-app/src/world_sim/{npcs,editing}.rs`/`variants.rs`/`app/mod.rs` (the runtime feeds) | done (M5-SEED-1 — the framework + the drunk/FTW/anniversary/constant arms + the remix flags/spawn; classic worlds byte-identical; ANALYSIS "Secret seeds (M5-SEED-1)". Deferred: the remix inverted terrain, the hive world, the drunk per-blob ore re-rolls, `ImproveAllChestContents`, the FTW/AI family = runtime follow-ups, the micro seeds = M5-SEED-2) |

## Ported by this slice (do not re-implement)

- `Player.PickupItem`'s stacking, the `maxStack` clamp and the world-surplus
  rule (`world_sim/items.rs`).
- `WorldItem`'s air gravity/friction/rest (`item.rs`).
- `KillTile` в†’ item drop for the soil/block/plant tables (`registry::tile_drop`
  + `break_tile`).
- Item в†’ tile placement with the one-item consume (`use_tile_item`).

## NOT PORTED (this slice's out-of-slice list, each its own row/task)

1. **Doors** вЂ” `WorldGen.OpenDoor`/`CloseDoor`/`ChangeDoorState`
   (`WorldGen.cs:37710-37890`), `Player.OpenDoor`/`HitDoor`, the `TileID.Sets.OpenDoorID`
   pair and the door `Check*` cascade: no door state machine exists in the port;
   tiles 10/11 only break/place.
2. **Chests** вЂ” `Chest.OpenChest`/`ChestUI` (`Chest.cs:100-260`, `Main.cs:20300+`):
   `chest_open`/loot windows, the `chest` field write on click and the
   `chest_holds_items` break refusal are present as a stub, the UI is not.
3. **Torch presentation** вЂ” `Player.ItemCheck_EmitHeldItemLight`, the wall-torch
   variants (`ItemID.Sets.TorchStyle`), `TileObjectData` torch support checks and
   the flicker light rows: the placed tile carries no light source in the port.
4. **Item ownership / grab delay in MP** вЂ” `NewItemOwnership`,
   `playerIndexTheItemIsReservedFor`, `WorldItem.DefaultGrabDelay = 100`
   (`WorldItem.cs:110`): the port has the host-side reservation map only.
5. **Grab-range accessories** вЂ” `goldRing`/`manaMagnet`/`lifeMagnet`/
   `treasureMagnet` and the star/heart/mana pickup arms of `PickupItem`
   (`Player.cs:35055-35099`).
6. **Death drops** вЂ” `Player.DropItems`/`DropItemsOnDeath` (`Player.cs:54302+`),
   `DropCoins`, the tombstone spawn: death currently drops nothing.
7. **Item.NewItem's spawn velocity spread** вЂ” the port pops at
   `vx в€€ [-1.4, 1.4]`, `vy в€€ [-3.4, -2.2]` instead of the C#'s
   `rand(-30..31)*0.1` / `rand(-40..-15)*0.1` (`Item.cs:49740-49746`).
8. **Slopes, conveyors and wet items** вЂ” `Collision.SlopeCollision`, the
   conveyor push (ported), the wet/honey/shimmer gravity rows
   (`WorldItem.cs:380-396`): the drop physics is the air case.
9. **Prefix merge refinement** вЂ” `Item.CanStack` is prefix equality; the
   `favorited`/`uniqueStack`/coin-merge (`DoCoins`) and the emergency
   stacking of `PickupReplacementTime` are app-side or unported.
10. **Sell/buy of drops** вЂ” `Player.SellItem`'s per-item math lives in
    `shop.rs` (S1-C-11); the world-drop `GetItemExpectedPrice` path is untouched.
